are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    82\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    83\t    \"\"\"\n    84\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    85\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    86\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    87\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    88\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    89\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    90\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    91\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    92\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    93\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    94\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    95\t\n    96\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    97\t        self.it = 0\n    98\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    99\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   100\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   101\t\n   102\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   103\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   104\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   105\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   106\t\n   107\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   112\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   113\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   114\t 131\t\n   132\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   133\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   134\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   135\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   136\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   137\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   138\t\n   139\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   140\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   141\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   142\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   143\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   144\t\n   145\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   146\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def _reflect(self, x, lb, ub, rng):\n    81\t        \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound values back into [lb, ub] with a random fallback.\n    82\t\n    83\t        Modulo-based reflection maps any real value into the feasible box while\n    84\t        preserving the periodicity of the search space; for degenerate cases it\n    85\t        falls back to uniform sampling inside the box.\n    86\t        \"\"\"\n    87\t        y = x.copy()\n    88\t        for j in range(self.dim):\n    89\t            lo = float(lb[j])\n    90\t            hi = float(ub[j])\n    91\t            span = hi lo + offset\n    97\t                if y[j] < lo or y[j] > hi:\n    98\t                    y[j] = lo + rng.random() * span\n    99\t            elif y[j] > hi:\n   100\t                offset = (y[j] - hi) % span\n   101\t                y[j] = hi - offset\n   102\t                if y[j] < lo or y[j] > hi:\n   103\t                    y[j] = lo + rng.random() * span\n   104\t        return y\n   105\t\n   106\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n   107\t        if self.controller is not None:\n   108\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n   109\t\n   110\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n   111\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n   112\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n   113\t        \n   114\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n   115\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n   116\t\n   117\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n   118\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   119\t        \n   120\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   121\t        evals = 0\n   122\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   123\t        \n   124\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   125\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   126\t                break\n   127\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   128\t            evals += 1\n   129\t\n   130\t        # Track best\n   131\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   132\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   133\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   134\t        \n   135\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   136\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   137\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   138\t\n   139\t        # Main loop\n   140\t        iteration = 0\n   141\t        done = False\n   142\t        \n   143\t        while not done:\n   144\t            iteration += 1\n   145\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   146\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   147\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   148\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   149\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   150\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   151\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   152\t            \n   153\t            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   154\t            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   155\t            \n   156\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   157\t            \n   158\t            # Generate trial vectors\n   159\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   160\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   161\t                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\n   162\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   163\t                if len(candidates) < 3:\n   164\t                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\n   165\t                else:\n   166\t                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n   167\t                \n   168\t                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n   169\t                \n   170\t                # Mutation and crossover\n   171\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   172\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   173\t                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\n   174\t                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\n   175\t                \n   176\t                # Boundary handling: clip\n   177\t                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\n   178\t            \n   179\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   180\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   181\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   182\t                    done = True\n   183\t                    break\n   184\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   185\t                evals += 1\n   186\t                \n   187\t                # Greedy selection\n   188\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   189\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   190\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   191\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   192\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   193\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   194\t            \n   195\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   196\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   197\t            \n   198\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   199\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   200\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   201\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   202\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   203\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   204\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   205\t            \n   206\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   207\t\n   208\t        self.stats = {\n   209\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   210\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   211\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   212\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   213\t x1: random distinct individual from current population.\\n                candidates1 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                x1_idx = int(candidates1[rng.randint(len(candidates1))])\\n\\n                # Update F/CR memory using the Lehmer mean (SHADE).\\n            if success_F:\\n                weights = np.asarray(success_delta, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = weights.sum()\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(weights)\\n                    total_w = float(len(weights))\\n                w = weights / total_w\\n\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                scr = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F: sum(w * F^2) / sum(w * F)\\n                mean_F = float(np.sum(w * sf * sf) / max(1e-12, np.sum(w * sf)))\\n                mean_CR = float(np.sum(w * scr))\\n                memory_F[mem_pos] = mean_F\\n                memory_CR[mem_pos] = mean_CR\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # ===== JADE/SHADE-inspired enhancements =====\\n        # External archive stores discarded parent vectors to improve diversity.\\n        archive = []\\n        archive_capacity = self.pop_size\\n\\n        # Success-history memory for adaptive F and CR (SHADE-style).\\n        memory_size = 5\\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, float(self.F))\\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, float(self.CR))\\n        mem_pos = 0\\n\\n        # Use 10% pbest vectors for exploitation-aware mutation.\\n        pbest_prob = 0.1\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller/static parameters are used as the base for adaptation.\\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # L-SHADE style population reduction: roughly halve the population\\n            # after half the evaluation budget is consumed, keeping the best half.\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals * 0.5 and pop_size > 10:\\n                new_pop_size = max(6, pop_size // 2)\\n                order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n                pop = pop[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                fitness = fitness[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                pop_size = new_pop_size\\n                if len(archive) > archive_capacity:\\n                    archive = archive[-archive_capacity:]\\n\\n            # Generate trial vectors with current-to-pbest/1 mutation + binomial crossover\\n            new_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            success_delta = []\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_count = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_prob))\\n\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Adapt F from memory (cauchy) with fallback to base F.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muF = memory_F[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    F_i = float(muF + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\\n                    if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                        F_i = 0.1\\n                    elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                        F_i = 1.0\\n                else:\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.1, 1.0))\\n\\n                # Adapt CR from memory (normal) with fallback to base CR.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muCR = memory_CR[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(muCR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n\\n                # pbest vector: uniformly random from the best pbest_count individuals.\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(pbest_count)])\\n                if pbest_idx == i:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(order[0])\\n\\n                # x1: random distinct individual from current population.\\n                candidates1 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                x1_idx = int(candidates1[rng.randint(len(candidates1))])\\n\\n                # x2: random distinct vector from population + archive (excluding current).\\n                combined = list(range(pop_size)) + list(range(len(archive)))\\n                combined = [idx for idx in combined if not (idx < pop_size and idx == i)]\\n                j2 = int(combined[rng.randint(len(combined))])\\n                if j2 < pop_size:\\n                    x2 = pop[j2]\\n                else:\\n                    x2 = archive[j2 - pop_size]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[x1_idx] - x2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover with reflection-based boundary handling.\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\\n                trial[mask] = v[mask]\\n                new_pop[i] = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub, rng)\\n\\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and perform JADE-style selection with archive.\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent (external archive in JADE).\\n                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\\n                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\\n                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    old_f = fitness[i]\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    # Record parameters for the success-history memory update.\\n                    success_F.append(base_F if base_F > 0 else 0.5)\\n                    success_CR.append(base_CR)\\n                    success_delta.append(abs(old_f - trial_f))\\n\\n            # Update F/CR memory using the Lehmer mean (SHADE).\\n            if success_F:\\n                weights = np.asarray(success_delta, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = weights.sum()\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(weights)\\n                    total_w = float(len(weights))\\n                w = weights / total_w\\n\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                scr = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F: sum(w * F^2) / sum(w * F)\\n                mean_F = float(np.sum(w * sf * sf) / max(1e-12, np.sum(w * sf)))\\n                mean_CR = float(np.sum(w * scr))\\n                memory_F[mem_pos] = mean_F\\n                memory_CR[mem_pos] = mean_CR\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n}\n</json>"

L-SHADE style population reduction: roughly halve the population\\n            # after half the evaluation budget is consumed, keeping the best half.\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals * 0.5 and pop_size > 10:\\n                new_pop_size = max(6, pop_size // 2)\\n                order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n                pop = pop[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                fitness = fitness[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                pop_size = new_pop_size\\n                if len(archive) > archive_capacity:\\n                    archive = archive[-archive_capacity:]\\n\\n            # Generate trial vectors with current-to-pbest/1 mutation + binomial crossover\\n            new_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            success_delta = []\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_count = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_prob))\\n\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Adapt F from memory (cauchy) with fallback to base F.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muF = memory_F[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    F_i = float(muF + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\\n                    if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                        F_i = 0.1\\n                    elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                        F_i = 1.0\\n                else:\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.1, 1.0))\\n\\n                # Adapt CR from memory (normal) with fallback to base CR.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muCR = memory_CR[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(muCR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n\\n                # pbest vector: uniformly random from the best pbest_count individuals.\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(pbest_count)])\\n                if pbest_idx == i:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(order[0])\\n\\n                # x1: random distinct individual from current population.\\n                candidates1 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                x1_idx = int(candidates1[rng.randint(len(candidates1))])\\n\\n                # x2: random distinct vector from population + archive (excluding current).\\n                combined = list(range(pop_size)) + list(range(len(archive)))\\n                combined = [idx for idx in combined if not (idx < pop_size and idx == i)]\\n                j2 = int(combined[rng.randint(len(combined))])\\n                if j2 < pop_size:\\n                    x2 = pop[j2]\\n                else:\\n                    x2 = archive[j2 - pop_size]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[x1_idx] - x2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover with reflection-based boundary handling.\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\\n                trial[mask] = v[mask]\\n                new_pop[i] = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub, rng)\\n\\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and perform JADE-style selection with archive.\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent (external archive in JADE).\\n                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\\n                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\\n                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    old_f = fitness[i]\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    # Record parameters for the success-history memory update.\\n                    success_F.append(base_F if base_F > 0 else 0.5)\\n                    success_CR.append(base_CR)\\n                    success_delta.append(abs(old_f - trial_f))\\n\\n            # Update F/CR memory using the Lehmer mean (SHADE).\\n            if success_F:\\n                weights = np.asarray(success_delta, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = weights.sum()\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(weights)\\n                    total_w = float(len(weights))\\n                w = weights / total_w\\n\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                scr = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F: sum(w * F^2) / sum(w * F)\\n                mean_F = float(np.sum(w * sf * sf) / max(1e-12, np.sum(w * sf)))\\n                mean_CR = float(np.sum(w * scr))\\n                memory_F[mem_pos] = mean_F\\n                memory_CR[mem_pos] = mean_CR\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"The file /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py has been edited. Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\", 'new_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # ===== JADE/SHADE-inspired enhancements =====\\n        # External archive stores discarded parent vectors to improve diversity.\\n        archive = []\\n        archive_capacity = self.pop_size\\n\\n        # Success-history memory for adaptive F and CR (SHADE-style).\\n        memory_size = 5\\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, float(self.F))\\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, float(self.CR))\\n        mem_pos = 0\\n\\n        # Use 10% pbest vectors for exploitation-aware mutation.\\n        pbest_prob = 0.1\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller/static parameters are used as the base for adaptation.\\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # L-SHADE style population reduction: roughly halve the population\\n            # after half the evaluation budget is consumed, keeping the best half.\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals * 0.5 and pop_size > 10:\\n                new_pop_size = max(6, pop_size // 2)\\n                order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n                pop = pop[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                fitness = fitness[order[:new_pop_size]]\\n                pop_size = new_pop_size\\n                if len(archive) > archive_capacity:\\n                    archive = archive[-archive_capacity:]\\n\\n            # Generate trial vectors with current-to-pbest/1 mutation + binomial crossover\\n            new_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            success_delta = []\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_count = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_prob))\\n\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Adapt F from memory (cauchy) with fallback to base F.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muF = memory_F[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    F_i = float(muF + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\\n                    if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                        F_i = 0.1\\n                    elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                        F_i = 1.0\\n                else:\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.1, 1.0))\\n\\n                # Adapt CR from memory (normal) with fallback to base CR.\\n                if rng.random() < 0.9:\\n                    muCR = memory_CR[int(rng.randint(memory_size))]\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(muCR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n\\n                # pbest vector: uniformly random from the best pbest_count individuals.\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(pbest_count)])\\n                if pbest_idx == i:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(order[0])\\n\\n                # x1: random distinct individual from current population.\\n                candidates1 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                x1_idx = int(candidates1[rng.randint(len(candidates1))])\\n\\n                # x2: random distinct vector from population + archive (excluding current).\\n                combined = list(range(pop_size)) + list(range(len(archive)))\\n                combined = [idx for idx in combined if not (idx < pop_size and idx == i)]\\n                j2 = int(combined[rng.randint(len(combined))])\\n                if j2 < pop_size:\\n                    x2 = pop[j2]\\n                else:\\n                    x2 = archive[j2 - pop_size]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[x1_idx] - x2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover with reflection-based boundary handling.\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\\n                trial[mask] = v[mask]\\n                new_pop[i] = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub, rng)\\n\\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and perform JADE-style selection with archive.\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent (external archive in JADE).\\n                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\\n                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\\n                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    old_f = fitness[i]\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    # Record parameters for the success-history memory update.\\n                    success_F.append(base_F if base_F > 0 else 0.5)\\n                    success_CR.append(base_CR)\\n                    success_delta.append(abs(old_f - trial_f))\\n\\n            # Update F/CR memory using the Lehmer mean (SHADE).\\n            if success_F:\\n                weights = np.asarray(success_delta, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = weights.sum()\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(weights)\\n                    total_w = float(len(weights))\\n                w = weights / total_w\\n\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                scr = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F: sum(w * F^2) / sum(w * F)\\n                mean_F = float(np.sum(w * sf * sf) / max(1e-12, np.sum(w * sf)))\\n                mean_CR = float(np.sum(w * scr))\\n                memory_F[mem_pos] = mean_F\\n                memory_CR[mem_pos] = mean_CR\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"The file /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py has been edited.    10\t\n    11\t# Import global configuration\n    12\timport sys\n    13\timport os\n    14\ttry:\n    15\t    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    16\t    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    17\t    # Set global seed at module load time\n    18\t    set_global_seed()\n    19\texcept Exception:\n    20\t    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    21\t    SEED = 42\n    22\t    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n    23\t        pass\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\tclass ParamController(ABC):\n    27\t    @abstractmethod\n    28\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    29\t        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n    30\t\n    31\t    @abstractmethod\n    32\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    33\t        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    37\t    def 42\t\n    43\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    44\t        return dict(self.params)\n    45\t\n    46\t\n    47\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    48\t    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    49\t    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    50\t    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    51\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    52\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    53\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    54\t\n    55\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    56\t        self.it = 0\n    57\t\n    58\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    59\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    60\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    61\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    62\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    63\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    64\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    65\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    66\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    67\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    68\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    69\t\n    70\t\n    71\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    72\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    73\t\n    74\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    75\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    76\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    77\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    78\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    79\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    80\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    81\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    82\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    83\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    84\t    \"\"\"\n    85\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    86\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    87\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    88\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    89\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    90\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    91\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    92\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    93\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    94\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    95\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    96\t\n    97\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    98\t        self.it = 0\n    99\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   100\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   101\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   102\t\n   103\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   104\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   105\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   106\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   109\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   110\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   111\t\n   112\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   113\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   114\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   115\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   117\t            else:\n   118\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   119\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   120\t\n   121\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   122\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   123\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   124\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t\n   127\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   128\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   129\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   130\t        else:\n   131\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   132\t\n   133\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   134\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   135\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   136\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   137\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   138\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   139\t\n   140\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   141\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   142\t        current_gbest_cv =
